statement = <sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql.psycopg.PGCompiler_psycopg object at 0x746159276f00>
parameters = [{'s': UUID('4413a1b2-c259-41f9-956b-fdc1fb5f3a50')}]

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    ) -> Self:
        """
        Execute a query or command to the database.
        """

            with self._conn.lock:
                self._conn.wait(

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    def test_e2e_event1_to_mock_sap_dua_approval_untuk_transfer(client):
        """Transfer operasional: dua approval (Head lalu Nutritionist) wajib → PO mock."""
        head = _mk_user("sppg_head", "Jakarta")
        nutritionist = _mk_user("sppg_nutritionist", "Jakarta")
        decision_id = _event1_decision_id()
        restore = _force_server_verdict("CONSISTENT")
        try:
            # propose
            resp = client.post(
                "/actions/propose",
                json={"decision_id": decision_id, "material_number": "MAT-TELUR"},
                headers=_login(client, head),
            )
            assert resp.status_code == 201, resp.text
            assert resp.json()["status"] == "pending_approval"
            # verify CONSISTENT — server-side chain override (kontrak O-SEC-3)
            resp = client.post(
                "/actions/verify",
                json={"decision_id": decision_id},
                headers=_login(client, head),
            )
            assert resp.status_code == 200, resp.text
            assert resp.json()["approval_requirement"]["min_approvals"] == 2
            # approve Head saja → belum cukup
            resp = client.post(
                "/actions/approve",
                json={"decision_id": decision_id, "approved": True},
                headers=_login(client, head),
            )
            assert resp.status_code == 200, resp.text
            resp = client.post(
                "/actions/execute",
                json={"decision_id": decision_id},
                headers=_login(client, head),
            )
            assert resp.status_code == 409, "approval Nutritionist belum ada"
            # approval kedua: Nutritionist
            resp = client.post(
                "/actions/approve",
                json={"decision_id": decision_id, "approved": True},

    @router.post("/actions/execute", status_code=200)
    async def execute_action(
        payload: ExecuteRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_roles(*PROPOSER_ROLES)),
        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    ):
        decision = await _load_decision(db, payload.decision_id)
        # Ruling 2026-10-14 §3: eksekusi terkunci ke lokasi target (semua tipe).
        _require_decision_scope(user, decision)
        # Audit M6 (18 Okt 2026): temuan review-only tidak pernah bisa dieksekusi.
        # Pesannya didahulukan supaya tidak terbaca "belum terverifikasi" — temuan
        # ini memang tidak menuntut verifikasi maupun approval.
        if decision.status == "review_only":
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="decision tinjauan (review-only) — tidak untuk dieksekusi",
            )
        if decision.status == "executed":
            if decision.decision_type in exclusions.ADMIN_TYPES:
                return {
                    "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                    "status": "executed",
                    "data_source": "LOCAL",
                    "exclusion": exclusions.serialize(await exclusions.for_decision(db, decision)),
                }
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision sudah dieksekusi")
        if _verifier_unverified(decision):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision belum terverifikasi")
        if decision.status == "rejected":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision ditolak")
        if not is_decision_valid(_now_utc(), decision.expires_at):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision expired")
        if decision.status not in {"pending_approval", "verifier_flagged", "approved"}:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail=f"status {decision.status} tidak dapat dieksekusi",
            )
    
        rows = (
            (await db.execute(select(Approval).where(Approval.decision_id == decision.id)))
            .scalars()
            .all()
        )
        approvers = await _approval_users(db, rows)
        evaluation = evaluate_approvals(
            _requirement_for(decision),
            [
                {"role": u.role, "approved": a.approved}
                for a, u in zip(rows, approvers)
            ],
        )
        if not evaluation["satisfied"]:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="approval belum memenuhi requirement (missing/rejected)",
            )
        # Ruling 2026-10-14 §3: hanya approval dari lokasi TARGET yang dihitung.
        # Filter ini dulu hanya dipakai untuk tipe administrasi, sehingga approval
        # approver SPPG lain (mis. baris lama sebelum ruling) ikut memenuhi matriks
        # keputusan operasional.
        scoped = evaluate_approvals(
            _requirement_for(decision),
            [
                {"role": u.role, "approved": a.approved}
                for a, u in zip(rows, approvers)
                if u.location_id == decision.target_location_id
            ],
        )
        if not scoped["satisfied"]:
            raise HTTPException(409, "approval scope tidak memenuhi requirement")
    
        if decision.decision_type in exclusions.ADMIN_TYPES:
            exclusion = await exclusions.execute(db, decision, user)
            decision.status = "executed"
            await append_block(
                db,
                action="execute",
                entity_id=decision.id,
                actor_id=user.id,
                payload={
                    "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                    "exclusion_id": str(exclusion.id),
                    "administrative_action": decision.decision_type,
                    "status": exclusion.status,
                },
            )
            await db.commit()
            return {
                "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                "status": "executed",
                "data_source": "LOCAL",
                "exclusion": exclusions.serialize(exclusion),
            }
    
        from app.services.supplier_eligibility import revalidate_transfer
    
        supplier_id = await revalidate_transfer(db, decision)
    
        # O-EVENT-2 revalidation: safety batch sumber dicek ULANG saat eksekusi
        # — rekomendasi lama tidak aman selamanya (plan §22). Fase 4: alasan
        # berasal dari evaluator yang sama dengan `can_execute`, jadi pesan 409
        # menyebut SEBAB yang benar (`safety_pending` ≠ `safety_fail`).
        from app.services.supplier_eligibility import PESAN_BLOKIR
    
        alasan_safety = await _source_batches_block_reason(db, decision)
        if alasan_safety is not None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail=(
                    f"safety revalidation gagal: "
                    f"{PESAN_BLOKIR.get(alasan_safety, alasan_safety)}"
                    " — decision tidak dapat dieksekusi"
                ),
            )
    
        # Snapshot utk PO
        commodity = (
            await db.execute(select(Commodity).where(Commodity.id == decision.commodity_id))
        ).scalar_one_or_none()
        if commodity is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="commodity decision tidak valid")
        qty = decision.quantity_kg or Decimal("0")
        if qty <= 0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="quantity decision tidak valid")
        # Fase 5 butir 2/4: harga PO = harga INVOICE (bahan + transportasi +
        # penanganan), bukan `sdc / qty`. Sebelum perbaikan ini `price` memuat
        # expected_loss + penalti kesegaran + penalti keamanan, sehingga dokumen
        # yang dibayar pemasok menagihkan kerugian internal yang tidak pernah ia
        # sebabkan (terukur: 37.171,00 vs 29.421,00 pada rincian uji Fase 5).
        breakdown = decision.cost_breakdown if isinstance(decision.cost_breakdown, dict) else None
>       biaya = commercial_cost(qty, breakdown)

    @router.post("/actions/execute", status_code=200)
    async def execute_action(
        payload: ExecuteRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_roles(*PROPOSER_ROLES)),
        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    ):
        decision = await _load_decision(db, payload.decision_id)
        # Ruling 2026-10-14 §3: eksekusi terkunci ke lokasi target (semua tipe).
        _require_decision_scope(user, decision)
        # Audit M6 (18 Okt 2026): temuan review-only tidak pernah bisa dieksekusi.
        # Pesannya didahulukan supaya tidak terbaca "belum terverifikasi" — temuan
        # ini memang tidak menuntut verifikasi maupun approval.
        if decision.status == "review_only":
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="decision tinjauan (review-only) — tidak untuk dieksekusi",
            )
        if decision.status == "executed":
            if decision.decision_type in exclusions.ADMIN_TYPES:
                return {
                    "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                    "status": "executed",
                    "data_source": "LOCAL",
                    "exclusion": exclusions.serialize(await exclusions.for_decision(db, decision)),
                }
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision sudah dieksekusi")
        if _verifier_unverified(decision):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision belum terverifikasi")
        if decision.status == "rejected":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision ditolak")
        if not is_decision_valid(_now_utc(), decision.expires_at):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision expired")
        if decision.status not in {"pending_approval", "verifier_flagged", "approved"}:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail=f"status {decision.status} tidak dapat dieksekusi",
            )
    
        rows = (
            (await db.execute(select(Approval).where(Approval.decision_id == decision.id)))
            .scalars()
            .all()
        )
        approvers = await _approval_users(db, rows)
        evaluation = evaluate_approvals(
            _requirement_for(decision),
            [
                {"role": u.role, "approved": a.approved}
                for a, u in zip(rows, approvers)
            ],
        )
        if not evaluation["satisfied"]:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="approval belum memenuhi requirement (missing/rejected)",
            )
        # Ruling 2026-10-14 §3: hanya approval dari lokasi TARGET yang dihitung.
        # Filter ini dulu hanya dipakai untuk tipe administrasi, sehingga approval
        # approver SPPG lain (mis. baris lama sebelum ruling) ikut memenuhi matriks
        # keputusan operasional.
        scoped = evaluate_approvals(
            _requirement_for(decision),
            [
                {"role": u.role, "approved": a.approved}
                for a, u in zip(rows, approvers)
                if u.location_id == decision.target_location_id
            ],
        )
        if not scoped["satisfied"]:
            raise HTTPException(409, "approval scope tidak memenuhi requirement")
    
        if decision.decision_type in exclusions.ADMIN_TYPES:
            exclusion = await exclusions.execute(db, decision, user)
            decision.status = "executed"
            await append_block(
                db,
                action="execute",
                entity_id=decision.id,
                actor_id=user.id,
                payload={
                    "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                    "exclusion_id": str(exclusion.id),
                    "administrative_action": decision.decision_type,
                    "status": exclusion.status,
                },
            )
            await db.commit()
            return {
                "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                "status": "executed",
                "data_source": "LOCAL",
                "exclusion": exclusions.serialize(exclusion),
            }
    
        from app.services.supplier_eligibility import revalidate_transfer
    
        supplier_id = await revalidate_transfer(db, decision)
    
        # O-EVENT-2 revalidation: safety batch sumber dicek ULANG saat eksekusi
        # — rekomendasi lama tidak aman selamanya (plan §22). Fase 4: alasan
        # berasal dari evaluator yang sama dengan `can_execute`, jadi pesan 409
        # menyebut SEBAB yang benar (`safety_pending` ≠ `safety_fail`).
        from app.services.supplier_eligibility import PESAN_BLOKIR

                status_code=409,
                detail=(
                    f"safety revalidation gagal: "
                    f"{PESAN_BLOKIR.get(alasan_safety, alasan_safety)}"

        # Snapshot utk PO

            await db.execute(select(Commodity).where(Commodity.id == decision.commodity_id))
        ).scalar_one_or_none()
        if commodity is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="commodity decision tidak valid")
        qty = decision.quantity_kg or Decimal("0")
        if qty <= 0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="quantity decision tidak valid")
        # Fase 5 butir 2/4: harga PO = harga INVOICE (bahan + transportasi +
        # penanganan), bukan `sdc / qty`. Sebelum perbaikan ini `price` memuat
        # expected_loss + penalti kesegaran + penalti keamanan, sehingga dokumen
        # yang dibayar pemasok menagihkan kerugian internal yang tidak pernah ia

        breakdown = decision.cost_breakdown if isinstance(decision.cost_breakdown, dict) else None

db = <sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio.session.AsyncSession object at 0x74615b78c140>

    @router.post("/actions/execute", status_code=200)
    async def execute_action(
        payload: ExecuteRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_roles(*PROPOSER_ROLES)),
        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    ):
        decision = await _load_decision(db, payload.decision_id)
        # Ruling 2026-10-14 §3: eksekusi terkunci ke lokasi target (semua tipe).
        _require_decision_scope(user, decision)
        # Audit M6 (18 Okt 2026): temuan review-only tidak pernah bisa dieksekusi.
        # Pesannya didahulukan supaya tidak terbaca "belum terverifikasi" — temuan
        # ini memang tidak menuntut verifikasi maupun approval.
        if decision.status == "review_only":
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="decision tinjauan (review-only) — tidak untuk dieksekusi",
            )
        if decision.status == "executed":
            if decision.decision_type in exclusions.ADMIN_TYPES:
                return {
                    "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                    "status": "executed",
                    "data_source": "LOCAL",
                    "exclusion": exclusions.serialize(await exclusions.for_decision(db, decision)),
                }
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision sudah dieksekusi")
        if _verifier_unverified(decision):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision belum terverifikasi")
        if decision.status == "rejected":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision ditolak")
        if not is_decision_valid(_now_utc(), decision.expires_at):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision expired")
        if decision.status not in {"pending_approval", "verifier_flagged", "approved"}:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail=f"status {decision.status} tidak dapat dieksekusi",
            )
    
        rows = (
            (await db.execute(select(Approval).where(Approval.decision_id == decision.id)))
            .scalars()
            .all()
        )
        approvers = await _approval_users(db, rows)
        evaluation = evaluate_approvals(
            _requirement_for(decision),
            [
                {"role": u.role, "approved": a.approved}
                for a, u in zip(rows, approvers)
            ],
        )
        if not evaluation["satisfied"]:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="approval belum memenuhi requirement (missing/rejected)",
            )
        # Ruling 2026-10-14 §3: hanya approval dari lokasi TARGET yang dihitung.
        # Filter ini dulu hanya dipakai untuk tipe administrasi, sehingga approval
        # approver SPPG lain (mis. baris lama sebelum ruling) ikut memenuhi matriks
        # keputusan operasional.
        scoped = evaluate_approvals(
            _requirement_for(decision),
            [
                {"role": u.role, "approved": a.approved}
                for a, u in zip(rows, approvers)
                if u.location_id == decision.target_location_id
            ],
        )
        if not scoped["satisfied"]:
            raise HTTPException(409, "approval scope tidak memenuhi requirement")
    
        if decision.decision_type in exclusions.ADMIN_TYPES:
            exclusion = await exclusions.execute(db, decision, user)
            decision.status = "executed"
            await append_block(
                db,
                action="execute",
                entity_id=decision.id,
                actor_id=user.id,
                payload={
                    "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                    "exclusion_id": str(exclusion.id),
                    "administrative_action": decision.decision_type,
                    "status": exclusion.status,
                },
            )
            await db.commit()
            return {
                "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                "status": "executed",
                "data_source": "LOCAL",
                "exclusion": exclusions.serialize(exclusion),
            }
    
        from app.services.supplier_eligibility import revalidate_transfer
    
        supplier_id = await revalidate_transfer(db, decision)
    
        # O-EVENT-2 revalidation: safety batch sumber dicek ULANG saat eksekusi
        # — rekomendasi lama tidak aman selamanya (plan §22). Fase 4: alasan
        # berasal dari evaluator yang sama dengan `can_execute`, jadi pesan 409
        # menyebut SEBAB yang benar (`safety_pending` ≠ `safety_fail`).
        from app.services.supplier_eligibility import PESAN_BLOKIR
    
        alasan_safety = await _source_batches_block_reason(db, decision)
        if alasan_safety is not None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail=(
                    f"safety revalidation gagal: "
                    f"{PESAN_BLOKIR.get(alasan_safety, alasan_safety)}"
                    " — decision tidak dapat dieksekusi"
                ),
            )
    
        # Snapshot utk PO
        commodity = (
            await db.execute(select(Commodity).where(Commodity.id == decision.commodity_id))
        ).scalar_one_or_none()
        if commodity is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="commodity decision tidak valid")
        qty = decision.quantity_kg or Decimal("0")
        if qty <= 0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="quantity decision tidak valid")
        # Fase 5 butir 2/4: harga PO = harga INVOICE (bahan + transportasi +
        # penanganan), bukan `sdc / qty`. Sebelum perbaikan ini `price` memuat
        # expected_loss + penalti kesegaran + penalti keamanan, sehingga dokumen
        # yang dibayar pemasok menagihkan kerugian internal yang tidak pernah ia
        # sebabkan (terukur: 37.171,00 vs 29.421,00 pada rincian uji Fase 5).
        breakdown = decision.cost_breakdown if isinstance(decision.cost_breakdown, dict) else None
>       biaya = commercial_cost(qty, breakdown)

    @router.post("/actions/execute", status_code=200)
    async def execute_action(
        payload: ExecuteRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_roles(*PROPOSER_ROLES)),
        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    ):
        decision = await _load_decision(db, payload.decision_id)
        # Ruling 2026-10-14 §3: eksekusi terkunci ke lokasi target (semua tipe).
        _require_decision_scope(user, decision)
        # Audit M6 (18 Okt 2026): temuan review-only tidak pernah bisa dieksekusi.
        # Pesannya didahulukan supaya tidak terbaca "belum terverifikasi" — temuan
        # ini memang tidak menuntut verifikasi maupun approval.
        if decision.status == "review_only":
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="decision tinjauan (review-only) — tidak untuk dieksekusi",
            )
        if decision.status == "executed":
            if decision.decision_type in exclusions.ADMIN_TYPES:
                return {
                    "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                    "status": "executed",
                    "data_source": "LOCAL",
                    "exclusion": exclusions.serialize(await exclusions.for_decision(db, decision)),
                }
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision sudah dieksekusi")
        if _verifier_unverified(decision):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision belum terverifikasi")
        if decision.status == "rejected":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision ditolak")
        if not is_decision_valid(_now_utc(), decision.expires_at):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision expired")
        if decision.status not in {"pending_approval", "verifier_flagged", "approved"}:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail=f"status {decision.status} tidak dapat dieksekusi",
            )
    
        rows = (
            (await db.execute(select(Approval).where(Approval.decision_id == decision.id)))
            .scalars()
            .all()
        )
        approvers = await _approval_users(db, rows)
        evaluation = evaluate_approvals(
            _requirement_for(decision),
            [
                {"role": u.role, "approved": a.approved}
                for a, u in zip(rows, approvers)
            ],
        )
        if not evaluation["satisfied"]:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="approval belum memenuhi requirement (missing/rejected)",
            )
        # Ruling 2026-10-14 §3: hanya approval dari lokasi TARGET yang dihitung.
        # Filter ini dulu hanya dipakai untuk tipe administrasi, sehingga approval
        # approver SPPG lain (mis. baris lama sebelum ruling) ikut memenuhi matriks
        # keputusan operasional.
        scoped = evaluate_approvals(
            _requirement_for(decision),
            [
                {"role": u.role, "approved": a.approved}
                for a, u in zip(rows, approvers)
                if u.location_id == decision.target_location_id
            ],
        )
        if not scoped["satisfied"]:
            raise HTTPException(409, "approval scope tidak memenuhi requirement")
    
        if decision.decision_type in exclusions.ADMIN_TYPES:
            exclusion = await exclusions.execute(db, decision, user)
            decision.status = "executed"
            await append_block(
                db,
                action="execute",
                entity_id=decision.id,
                actor_id=user.id,
                payload={
                    "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                    "exclusion_id": str(exclusion.id),
                    "administrative_action": decision.decision_type,
                    "status": exclusion.status,
                },
            )
            await db.commit()
            return {
                "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                "status": "executed",
                "data_source": "LOCAL",
                "exclusion": exclusions.serialize(exclusion),
            }
    
        from app.services.supplier_eligibility import revalidate_transfer
    
        supplier_id = await revalidate_transfer(db, decision)

        # berasal dari evaluator yang sama dengan `can_execute`, jadi pesan 409
        # menyebut SEBAB yang benar (`safety_pending` ≠ `safety_fail`).

        if alasan_safety is not None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail=(
                    f"safety revalidation gagal: "
                    f"{PESAN_BLOKIR.get(alasan_safety, alasan_safety)}"
                    " — decision tidak dapat dieksekusi"
                ),
            )

            await db.execute(select(Commodity).where(Commodity.id == decision.commodity_id))
        ).scalar_one_or_none()
        if commodity is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="commodity decision tidak valid")
        qty = decision.quantity_kg or Decimal("0")
        if qty <= 0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="quantity decision tidak valid")
        # Fase 5 butir 2/4: harga PO = harga INVOICE (bahan + transportasi +
        # penanganan), bukan `sdc / qty`. Sebelum perbaikan ini `price` memuat
        # expected_loss + penalti kesegaran + penalti keamanan, sehingga dokumen

payload = ExecuteRequest(decision_id=UUID('1641c192-1bfa-4253-92b4-78dd4548e188'))
user = <app.models.users.User object at 0x7461597dfe60>
db = <sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio.session.AsyncSession object at 0x7461597dff20>

    
    @router.post("/actions/execute", status_code=200)
    async def execute_action(
        payload: ExecuteRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_roles(*PROPOSER_ROLES)),
        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    ):
        decision = await _load_decision(db, payload.decision_id)
        # Ruling 2026-10-14 §3: eksekusi terkunci ke lokasi target (semua tipe).
        _require_decision_scope(user, decision)
        # Audit M6 (18 Okt 2026): temuan review-only tidak pernah bisa dieksekusi.
        # Pesannya didahulukan supaya tidak terbaca "belum terverifikasi" — temuan
        # ini memang tidak menuntut verifikasi maupun approval.
        if decision.status == "review_only":
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="decision tinjauan (review-only) — tidak untuk dieksekusi",
            )
        if decision.status == "executed":
            if decision.decision_type in exclusions.ADMIN_TYPES:
                return {
                    "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                    "status": "executed",
                    "data_source": "LOCAL",
                    "exclusion": exclusions.serialize(await exclusions.for_decision(db, decision)),
                }
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision sudah dieksekusi")
        if _verifier_unverified(decision):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision belum terverifikasi")
        if decision.status == "rejected":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision ditolak")
        if not is_decision_valid(_now_utc(), decision.expires_at):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="decision expired")
        if decision.status not in {"pending_approval", "verifier_flagged", "approved"}:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail=f"status {decision.status} tidak dapat dieksekusi",
            )
    
        rows = (
            (await db.execute(select(Approval).where(Approval.decision_id == decision.id)))
            .scalars()
            .all()
        )
        approvers = await _approval_users(db, rows)
        evaluation = evaluate_approvals(
            _requirement_for(decision),
            [
                {"role": u.role, "approved": a.approved}
                for a, u in zip(rows, approvers)
            ],
        )
        if not evaluation["satisfied"]:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="approval belum memenuhi requirement (missing/rejected)",
            )
        # Ruling 2026-10-14 §3: hanya approval dari lokasi TARGET yang dihitung.
        # Filter ini dulu hanya dipakai untuk tipe administrasi, sehingga approval
        # approver SPPG lain (mis. baris lama sebelum ruling) ikut memenuhi matriks
        # keputusan operasional.
        scoped = evaluate_approvals(
            _requirement_for(decision),
            [
                {"role": u.role, "approved": a.approved}
                for a, u in zip(rows, approvers)
                if u.location_id == decision.target_location_id
            ],
        )
        if not scoped["satisfied"]:
            raise HTTPException(409, "approval scope tidak memenuhi requirement")
    
        if decision.decision_type in exclusions.ADMIN_TYPES:
            exclusion = await exclusions.execute(db, decision, user)
            decision.status = "executed"
            await append_block(
                db,
                action="execute",
                entity_id=decision.id,
                actor_id=user.id,
                payload={
                    "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                    "exclusion_id": str(exclusion.id),
                    "administrative_action": decision.decision_type,
                    "status": exclusion.status,
                },
            )
            await db.commit()
            return {
                "decision_id": str(decision.id),
                "status": "executed",
                "data_source": "LOCAL",
                "exclusion": exclusions.serialize(exclusion),
            }
    
        from app.services.supplier_eligibility import revalidate_transfer
    
        supplier_id = await revalidate_transfer(db, decision)
    
        # O-EVENT-2 revalidation: safety batch sumber dicek ULANG saat eksekusi
        # — rekomendasi lama tidak aman selamanya (plan §22). Fase 4: alasan
        # berasal dari evaluator yang sama dengan `can_execute`, jadi pesan 409
        # menyebut SEBAB yang benar (`safety_pending` ≠ `safety_fail`).
        from app.services.supplier_eligibility import PESAN_BLOKIR
    
        alasan_safety = await _source_batches_block_reason(db, decision)
        if alasan_safety is not None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail=(
                    f"safety revalidation gagal: "
                    f"{PESAN_BLOKIR.get(alasan_safety, alasan_safety)}"
                    " — decision tidak dapat dieksekusi"
                ),
            )
    
        # Snapshot utk PO
        commodity = (
            await db.execute(select(Commodity).where(Commodity.id == decision.commodity_id))
        ).scalar_one_or_none()
        if commodity is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="commodity decision tidak valid")
        qty = decision.quantity_kg or Decimal("0")
        if qty <= 0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="quantity decision tidak valid")
        # Fase 5 butir 2/4: harga PO = harga INVOICE (bahan + transportasi +
        # penanganan), bukan `sdc / qty`. Sebelum perbaikan ini `price` memuat
        # expected_loss + penalti kesegaran + penalti keamanan, sehingga dokumen
        # yang dibayar pemasok menagihkan kerugian internal yang tidak pernah ia
        # sebabkan (terukur: 37.171,00 vs 29.421,00 pada rincian uji Fase 5).
>       breakdown = decision.cost_breakdown if isinstance(decision.cost_breakdown, dict) else None
E       NameError: name 'commercial_cost' is not defined

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("binding", ["explicit", "missing"])
    def test_approval_summary_or_complete_then_execute_parity(client, binding):
        """Only a bound transfer promises a supplier and can execute."""
        batch = _mk_batch(_bogor_id(), fail=False) if binding == "explicit" else None
        # Kasus "missing" menuntut lokasi+komoditas yang TIDAK punya satu pun batch,
        # supaya pemasok tidak bisa disimpulkan. Dulu dipaku ke `ayam` + Jakarta;
        # seed bertambah, jadi pasangannya DICARI saat tes berjalan.
        pasangan = None if batch else _pasangan_tanpa_batch(_jakarta_id())
        if not batch and pasangan is None:
            # Premis kasus ini (pasangan lokasi×komoditas TANPA batch) bisa hilang
            # ketika seed bertambah — generator aktivitas demo mengisi seluruh
            # pasangan 10 lokasi × 3 komoditas. Itu bukan regresi produk, jadi
            # dilewati dengan alasan jujur, bukan digagalkan.
            pytest.skip(
                "semua lokasi×komoditas punya batch; kasus supplier_unknown mustahil dibentuk"
            )
        if batch:
            did = _mk_decision(_bogor_id(), _jakarta_id())
        else:
            assert pasangan is not None  # dijamin skip di atas; ini hanya untuk tipe
            sumber, komoditas = pasangan
            did = _mk_decision(sumber, _jakarta_id(), commodity=komoditas)
        try:
            if batch:
                _bind_decision(did, batch)
            # Verifikasi + verdict konsisten (fixture DB level, server verdict).
            client.post(
                "/actions/verify", headers=_head_user(client), json={"decision_id": did}
            )
            _db_exec(
                "UPDATE decisions SET verifier_note='consistent', verified_by="
                "(SELECT id FROM users WHERE role='sppg_head' LIMIT 1) WHERE id=:d",
                {"d": did},
            )
            # Approval Head saja TIDAK cukup: transfer operasional butuh dua role
            # (PLAN.md §13.3) meski verdict verifier CONSISTENT.
            ra = client.post(
                "/actions/approve",
                headers=_head_user(client),
                json={"decision_id": did, "approved": True},
            )
            assert ra.status_code == 200, ra.text
    
            summary = client.get(
                f"/decisions/{did}/approval-summary", headers=_head_user(client)
            ).json()
            assert summary["approval_state"]["satisfied"] is False
            # Approval kedua: Nutritionist (user berbeda).
            rn = client.post(
                "/actions/approve",
                headers=_nut_user(client),
                json={"decision_id": did, "approved": True},
            )
            assert rn.status_code == 200, rn.text
            summary = client.get(
                f"/decisions/{did}/approval-summary", headers=_head_user(client)
            ).json()
            assert summary["approval_state"]["satisfied"] is True
            if binding == "missing":
                rx = client.post(
                    "/actions/execute", headers=_head_user(client), json={"decision_id": did}
                )
                assert rx.status_code == 409, rx.text
                assert "supplier_unknown" in rx.json()["detail"]
                assert summary["can_execute"] is False
                assert summary["supplier"] is None
                assert rx.json()["detail"] in summary["blocking_reasons"]
                assert not _db_all(
                    "SELECT id FROM sap_mock_purchase_orders WHERE decision_id=:d", {"d": did}
                )
                return

_______________________ test_seed_idempotent_dan_lengkap _______________________

    def test_seed_idempotent_dan_lengkap():
        engine = _db()
        try:
            with TestClient(app) as c:
                # Login user SPPG existing dari seed_data (punya location).
                email = "sppg.head@demo.local"
                with engine.begin() as conn:
                    row = conn.execute(
                        text("SELECT count(*) FROM users WHERE email = :e"),
                        {"e": email},
                    ).scalar()
                if row:
                    tok = c.post(
                        "/auth/login",
                        json={"email": email, "password": "Demo#SPPG2026"},
                    ).json()["access_token"]
                else:
                    tok = None
                auth_headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {tok}"} if tok else {}
    
                # Reset dulu (force) supaya test deterministik.
                r1 = c.post("/demo/seed", json={"force": True}, headers=auth_headers)
                assert r1.status_code == 200, r1.text
                assert r1.json()["seeded"] is True
                assert r1.json()["decisions"] == 7
    
                # Idempotent: seed ulang tanpa force → skip.
                r2 = c.post("/demo/seed", json={}, headers=auth_headers)
                assert r2.json()["seeded"] is False
    
                # Kasus lengkap + shape timeline benar.
                with engine.begin() as conn:
                    per_case = dict(
                        conn.execute(
                            text(
                                "SELECT d.demo_case, count(*) FROM decisions d "
                                "JOIN locations l ON l.id = d.target_location_id "
                                "WHERE d.demo_case IS NOT NULL AND l.name = 'Jakarta' "
                                "GROUP BY d.demo_case"
                            )
                        ).all()
                    )
                # Kasus skrip harus ada (boleh ada kasus tambahan dari seed lain,
                # mis. "bukti-hari-ini" milik tombol "Siapkan data demo").
                assert CASES <= set(per_case)
>               assert per_case["rejection-loop-kuota"] == 3
E               assert 6 == 3

    def test_event1_e2e_propose_approve_execute_mock_sap(client):
        """Full hop aktual: supervisor→decision→propose→dual approve→execute→PO mock."""
        head = _mk_user("sppg_head", "Jakarta")
        nut = _mk_user("sppg_nutritionist", "Jakarta")

        try:

            assert outcome["decision_snapshot"]["status"] == "proposed"
            assert outcome["verifier"]["verdict"] == "UNAVAILABLE"
            # trace tersimpan = langkah aktual
            steps = _trace_rows(decision_id)
            assert [s["step_name"] for s in steps] == [
                "DETECT", "VERIFY", "TRACE", "PREDICT", "OPTIMIZE", "DECIDE",
            ]
            assert all("LEARN" not in s["step_name"] for s in steps)

                    "material_number": "MAT-TELUR",
                    "supplier_id": None,
                },
                headers=head_h,
            )
            assert resp.status_code == 201, resp.text
            # UNAVAILABLE dari supervisor hop → approval TERKUNCI (audit M1: approve
            # menuntut verdict hasil audit). Verdict dicatat lewat jalur server
            # /actions/verify dengan provider uji, bukan dengan memalsukan kolom DB.

                resp = client.post(
                    "/actions/verify",
                    json={"decision_id": decision_id},
                    headers=head_h,
                )
                assert resp.status_code == 200, resp.text
                assert resp.json()["recorded"] is True, resp.text
            finally:
                restore()
            # dual AND tetap berlaku (konservatif, S3-01)
            assert resp.json()["approval_requirement"]["min_approvals"] == 2

    def test_event1_e2e_verifier_unavailable_dual_approval(client):
        """dual AND tetap wajib: 1 approval tidak cukup (409).
    
        Sejak audit M1, verify UNAVAILABLE tidak membuka approval sama sekali, jadi
        verdict dicatat lebih dulu lewat /actions/verify dengan provider uji — yang
        diuji di sini adalah kuorum dua peran, bukan jalur verifikasi.
        """
        head = _mk_user("sppg_head", "Jakarta")
        nut = _mk_user("sppg_nutritionist", "Jakarta")
        decision_id, _ = _event1_decision_id()
        try:
            client.post(
                "/actions/propose",
                json={
                    "decision_id": decision_id,
                    "material_number": "MAT-TELUR",
                    "supplier_id": None,
                },
                headers=_login(client, head),
            )
            restore = _force_server_verdict("CONSISTENT", "uji kuorum")
            try:
                resp = client.post(
                    "/actions/verify",
                    json={"decision_id": decision_id},
                    headers=_login(client, head),
                )
                assert resp.status_code == 200, resp.text
                assert resp.json()["recorded"] is True, resp.text
            finally:
                restore()
            # HANYA head approve → execute HARUS ditolak (dual AND)
            client.post(
                "/actions/approve",
                json={"decision_id": decision_id, "approved": True},
                headers=_login(client, head),
            )
            resp = client.post(
                "/actions/execute",
                json={"decision_id": decision_id},
                headers=_login(client, head),
            )
            assert resp.status_code == 409
            # KEDUA role approve → execute sukses + PO mock

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )

                )

    def test_decision_time_tersedia_setelah_eksekusi(client, monitor_headers):
        """Kartu Decision Time berhenti jadi `no_data` setelah satu eksekusi nyata.
    
        Metrik hanya menghitung keputusan ber-`demo_case IS NULL` yang sudah
        committed (PO ada) — keputusan Event 1 memenuhi keduanya setelah eksekusi.
        """
        head = _mk_user("sppg_head", "Jakarta")
        restore = _force_server_verdict("CONSISTENT")
        decision_id: str | None = None
        gizi_email: str | None = None
        try:
            before = _kpi_metric(client, monitor_headers, "Decision Time")
            # JANGAN mengandaikan DB kosong. DB kerja adalah DB DEMO yang dipakai
            # bersama: keputusan nyata yang sudah dieksekusi (beserta PO-nya) bisa
            # sudah ada di jendela metrik, sehingga keadaannya `available` sebelum
            # tes ini jalan. Yang diuji adalah TRANSISI: keputusan yang baru
            # dieksekusi ikut terhitung.
            sebelum_sampel = int(before.get("sample_count") or 0)
            decision_id, _po, gizi_email = _run_execute_flow(client, head)
            after = _kpi_metric(client, monitor_headers, "Decision Time")
            assert after["state"] == "available", after
            assert after["value"] is not None
            assert int(after["sample_count"]) == sebelum_sampel + 1, after
            assert float(after["value"]) >= 0
        finally:
            restore()

    def test_keputusan_tanpa_rincian_lengkap_menerbitkan_po_bertanda_legacy(client):
        """Rincian hanya `purchase` (bentuk data lama) → PO tetap terbit, harga =
        harga BAHAN saja, dan barisnya ditandai `legacy_unmigrated`.
    
        Kenapa tidak menolak (422): menolak eksekusi berarti mengubah kontrak alur
        yang dipakai ~10 berkas tes lain dan menghentikan operator pada data lama
        yang sah. Yang dilarang rencana adalah MENAGIHKAN penalti dan MENGARANG
        komponen — bukan menerbitkan PO dari komponen yang tercatat. Penandaan
        itulah yang memberi tahu pembaca rinciannya belum lengkap.
        """
        head = _mk_user("sppg_head", "Jakarta")
        nutritionist = _mk_user("sppg_nutritionist", "Jakarta")
        decision_id = None
        try:
            headers = _login(client, head)
            resp = client.post(
                "/decisions",
                json={
                    "recommendation": {
                        "candidate": {
                            "source_location": "Cianjur",
                            "destination_location": "Jakarta",
                            "commodity": "telur",
                            "quantity_kg": QTY,
                        },
                        # Hanya bahan: transportasi & penanganan tidak tercatat.
                        "safe_delivered_cost": "15720000.00",
                        "cost_breakdown": {"purchase": "15720000.00"},
                    }
                },
                headers=headers,
            )
            assert resp.status_code == 201, resp.text
            decision_id = resp.json()["decision_id"]
            _ikat_binding(decision_id)
            client.post("/actions/propose", json={"decision_id": decision_id}, headers=headers)
            _verify_decision(decision_id)
            for akun in (head, nutritionist):
                client.post(
                    "/actions/approve",
                    json={"decision_id": decision_id, "approved": True},
                    headers=_login(client, akun),
                )
>           resp = client.post(
                "/actions/execute", json={"decision_id": decision_id}, headers=headers
            )

____ test_fetch_excludes_later_harvests_without_advancing_as_of[live-race] _____

monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x74614a870e00>
explicit_as_of = False

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("explicit_as_of", [False, True], ids=["live-race", "historical"])
    def test_fetch_excludes_later_harvests_without_advancing_as_of(monkeypatch, explicit_as_of):
        engine = create_engine(
            DSN_SYNC
        )
    
        class FrozenClock(datetime):
            @classmethod
            def now(cls, tz=None):
                return AS_OF + timedelta(days=1) if explicit_as_of else AS_OF
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(rs, "datetime", FrozenClock)
        try:
            with engine.connect() as connection, connection.begin() as transaction:
                with Session(bind=connection) as session:
                    location = session.scalar(select(Location).where(Location.name == "Cianjur"))
                    commodity = session.scalar(select(Commodity).where(Commodity.name == "telur"))
                    supplier = session.scalar(select(Supplier).limit(1))
                    ids = {name: uuid.uuid4() for name in ("old", "boundary", "later")}
                    harvested = {
                        "old": AS_OF - timedelta(days=1),
                        "boundary": AS_OF,
                        "later": AS_OF + timedelta(milliseconds=600),
                    }
    
                    class LaterVisibleSession:
                        inserted = False
    
                        async def execute(self, statement):
                            if not self.inserted:
                                self.inserted = True
                                # Deterministic interleaving: these rows become visible only
                                # after fetch_operational_facts has captured its clock.
                                session.add_all([
                                    Batch(
                                        id=ids[name], location_id=location.id,
                                        commodity_id=commodity.id, supplier_id=supplier.id,
                                        quantity_kg=Decimal("10"), harvested_at=timestamp,
                                        # Bukti suhu lengkap: sejak F-02 (25 Okt 2026) log
                                        # kosong tidak lagi berbuah skor `pass` (0,7), dan
                                        # tes ini menguji pemotongan `as_of`, bukan freshness.
                                        temperature_log=_log_suhu(timestamp),
                                        safety_status="pass",
                                    )
                                    for name, timestamp in harvested.items()
                                ])
                                session.flush()
                            return session.execute(statement)
    
                    facts = asyncio.run(rs.fetch_operational_facts(
                        LaterVisibleSession(), AS_OF if explicit_as_of else None
                    ))
                    batches = {b["batch_id"]: b for b in facts[4]}
                    assert str(ids["later"]) not in batches
                    assert batches[str(ids["boundary"])]["freshness_score"] == Decimal("1.000000")
>                   expected = rs.compute_freshness_score(
                        harvested["old"], AS_OF, rs.settings.shelf_life_days_telur,
                        _log_suhu(harvested["old"]),
                        rs.settings.safe_temp_min_c, rs.settings.safe_temp_max_c,
                    )
